אומר אני אף בערכין כן – if it happens by chance, even with Valuations is similar to sacrifices, then it is like sacrifices, But it was stated [in this Mishnah] that Valuations are not like Sacrifices, because they are not similar one with the other, and for what reason does a poor person who dedicated the value of a rich person, gives the value of a poor person because of the law regarding the payment of certain vows according to one’s [own] means, because the rich person is not liable for anything, and not the monetary value of the Metzorah/leper. But this [individual] who spoke regarding the rich person, did not intend other than according to the measurement of the years of the rich individual which are less or more than his own years. Therefore, he is judged according to his own means/wealth, but the rich person who said: “My value is upon me,” that he is liable for a complete/full value, similar to the [wealthy] leper and the poor person heard it and said, “What that person that this upon me,” he gives the value of a rich person. This is the reading.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:2:2)


**הָכִי גָּרְסִינַן הָיָה עָנִי וְהֶעֱשִׁיר אוֹ עָשִׁיר וְהֶעֱנִי מְשַׁלֵּם עֵרֶךְ עָשִׁיר.** הָיָה עָנִי וְהֶעֱשִׁיר קֹדֶם נְתִינָה, מְשַׁלֵּם עֵרֶךְ עָשִׁיר, אֲשֶׁר תַּשִּׂיג יַד הַנּוֹדֵר אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא, וַהֲרֵי יָדוֹ מַשֶּׂגֶת. עָשִׁיר וְהֶעֱנִי נַמִּי תַּשִּׂיג יַד הַנּוֹדֵר כְּתִיב, וַהֲרֵי הָיְתָה מַשֶּׂגֶת בְּעֵת שֶׁנָּדַר:


היה עני והעשיר או עשיר והעני משלם ערך עשיר – if he was poor and became rich prior to giving [the valuation money], he pays the value of a rich person, for the All-Merciful one said (Leviticus 27:8): “according to what the vower can afford,” for it is in regard to one’s wealth/means. Rich and the poor [alike] also according to one’s means of the person who vows, is written (see the verse mentioned above), for he had the means at the time that he made the vow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:2:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אֲפִלּוּ עָנִי וְהֶעֱשִׁיר וְחָזַר וְהֶעֱנִי נוֹתֵן עֵרֶךְ עָשִׁיר.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְאִם מָךְ הוּא מֵעֶרְכְּךָ, עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בְמָכוּתוֹ מִתְּחִלָּתוֹ וְעַד סוֹפוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר אפילו עני והעשיר וחזר והעני נותן ערך עשיר – as it is written (Leviticus 27:8): “But if one cannot afford the equivalent,” until it will be that he must have remained in his impoverished condition from the beginning to the end of the proceedings (see Talmud Arakhin 17b). But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:3:1)


**אֲפִלּוּ אָבִיו מֵת.** בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁנִּתְחַיֵּב זֶה בְּקָרְבָּן הָיָה אָבִיו גּוֹסֵס וְנוֹטֶה לָמוּת, וּמֵת וְיָרַשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ רִבּוֹא קֹדֶם שֶׁהֵבִיא קָרְבָּנוֹ, אֵינוֹ מֵבִיא אֶלָּא קָרְבַּן עָנִי, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁנִּתְחַיֵּב בַּקָּרְבָּן:


אפילו אביו מת – at the time when this person is obligated for a sacrifice, his father is on his deathbed and about to die, and he (i.e., the father) died and he inherited from him ten-thousand before he brought his sacrifice, he does not bring anything other than the sacrifice of an impoverished person, just as he was at the time that he became obligated for a sacrifice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:3:2)


**סְפִינָתוֹ בַיָּם.** לֹא שֶׁהָיְתָה סְפִינָתוֹ טְעוּנָה מִסְּחוֹרוֹת שֶׁלּוֹ בְּרִבּוֹא, דְּאִם כֵּן עָשִׁיר הוּא. אֶלָּא שֶׁהָיְתָה סְפִינָתוֹ מֻשְׂכֶּרֶת לַאֲחֵרִים בְּרִבּוֹא שָׂכָר, וְהוּא אֵין בְּיָדוֹ אֶלָּא אוֹתָהּ סְפִינָה. וּמִשּׁוּם שְׂכָרָהּ לָאו עָשִׁיר הוּא, דְּאֵין שְׂכִירוּת מִשְׁתַּלֶּמֶת אֶלָּא בְּסוֹפָהּ, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁעַכְשָׁיו עָנִי הוּא. וּמִשּׁוּם הַסְּפִינָה עַצְמָהּ לָאו עָשִׁיר הוּא, דְּהַאי תַּנָּא סָבַר כְּמַאן דְּאָמַר לְקַמָּן שֶׁאִם הָיָה הַמַּעֲרִיךְ חַמָּר נוֹתֵן לוֹ הַכֹּהֵן חֲמוֹרוֹ וְאֵינוֹ נוֹטְלוֹ לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ, וְאִם הָיָה אִכָּר מַנִּיחַ לוֹ צֶמֶד בְּקָרוֹ דְּהִיא פַּרְנָסָתוֹ, וְהָכִי נַמִּי מַנִּיחַ לוֹ סְפִינָתוֹ:


ספינתו בים – not that his ship was lade from his business-dealings with ten-thousand, for if so, he is rich. But rather, that his ship was rented out to others with ten-thousand as payment, and he lacks anything in his hand other than that ship. But because of the payment, he is not rich, for the rent is not completely paid other than at the end, and it is found that now he is a poor person. But, because of the ship itself, he is a poor person, for this Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] holds like one who says further on (see Talmud Arakhin 17b-18a) that if the person making the Valuation was a donkey-driver, the Kohen gives him his donkey and he doesn’t give it to be dedicated to the Temple, but if he was a farmer, he leaves for him the yoke/pair of working animals tied to the yoke which is his income. And so too, he leaves him his ship.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:1)


**יֶלֶד.** מִבֶּן עֶשְׂרִים וְעַד בֶּן שִׁשִּׁים, דַּהֲוֵי עֶרְכּוֹ חֲמִשִּׁים שְׁקָלִים:


ילד – (who pledged the valuation of an elder) from age twenty until age sixty, for his value is fifty Shekel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:2)


**נוֹתֵן כִּזְמַן הָעֵרֶךְ.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) כְּעֶרְכְּךָ יָקוּם, אֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן אֶלָּא כִּזְמַן הָעֵרֶךְ:


נותן כזמן הערך – as it is written (Leviticus 27:17): “[If he consecrates his land as of the jubilee year,] its assessment stands,” he does not give other than according to the time of the valuation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:3)


**יוֹם שְׁלֹשִׁים כִּלְמַטָּה.** אִם אָמַר עֵרֶךְ פְּלוֹנִי קָטָן עָלָי, וְאוֹתוֹ קָטָן הָיָה בְּאוֹתוֹ יוֹם בֶּן שְׁלֹשִׁים, הָוֵי כִלְמַטָּה וְלֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם. דְּאֵין עֵרֶךְ לְפָחוּת מִבֶּן חֹדֶשׁ. דְּהָכִי כְּתִיב [שָׁם] וְאִם מִבֶּן חֹדֶשׁ:


יום שלשים כלמטה – if he stated that the valuation of so-and-so a minor child is upon me, and that minor was on that day was thirty [days old], it is like it is below that, and he did not say anything, for there is no valuation for less than a month [old], and thus it is written (Leviticus 27:6): “If the age is from one month [to five years, the equivalent for a male is five shekels of silver, and the equivalent for a female is three shekels of silver].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:4)


**מִבֶּן שִׁשִּׁים שָׁנָה וָמַעְלָה.** מַשְׁמַע שֶׁשָּׁלְמָה שְׁנַת שִׁשִּׁים וְאָז הוּא נִדּוֹן כְּשִׁשִּׁים, אֲבָל בִּשְׁנַת שִׁשִּׁים נִדּוֹן כְּיֶלֶד:


מבן ששים שנה ומעלה – implying that the sixtieth year was completed, and then he is judged like someone sixty [years old], but in the sixtieth year, he is judged as a child.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:5)


**הֵן אִם עָשִׂינוּ.** בִּתְמִיהָה. כְּלוֹמַר, וְכִי כֵן הוּא, אִם עָשִׂינוּ [שְׁנַת] שִׁשִּׁים כִּלְמַטָּה לְהַחֲמִיר דְּעֵרֶךְ גָּדוֹל יֵשׁ לְפָחוּת מִבֶּן שִׁשִּׁים חֲמִשִּׁים סֶלַע, וּלְיוֹתֵר מִבֶּן שִׁשִּׁים אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר:


הן אם עשינו – in astonishment. Meaning to say, and if this the case, if we made the sixtieth [year] like it is lower than this to be stringent for a valuation of an adult one should lessen from athe age of sixty for fifty Sela, but for someone older than age sixty, it is not other than fifteen [Sela].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:6)


**כִּלְמַטָּה מִמֶּנָּה לְהָקֵל.** דְּעֵרֶך יוֹתֵר עַל בֶּן עֶשְׂרִים, גָּדוֹל הוּא מִפָּחוּת מִבֶּן עֶשְׂרִים. וְכֵן בְּבֶן חָמֵשׁ. כְּדִכְתִיבֵי בִקְרָאֵי:


כלמטה ממנה להקל – that the valuation is more for someone who is twenty years old, an adult who is a less than twenty years, old, and similarly, with someone who is five [years old]. As it is written in Biblical verses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/4:4:7)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר.** לְעוֹלָם שְׁנַת חָמֵשׁ וּשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁנַת שִׁשִּׁים כִּלְמַטָּה, עַד שֶׁתִּשְׁלַם כָּל הַשָּׁנָה וְחֹדֶשׁ וְיוֹם אֶחָד מִשָּׁנָה הָאַחֶרֶת. דְּיָלֵיף גְּזֵרָה שָׁוָה לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה מִבְּכוֹר, נֶאֱמַר כָּאן מִבֶּן שִׁשִּׁים שָׁנָה וָמַעְלָה, וְנֶאֱמַר בִּבְכוֹר אָדָם (במדבר יח) וּפְדוּיָו מִבֶּן חֹדֶשׁ וָמַעְלָה, מַה לְּהַלָּן חֹדֶשׁ וְיוֹם אֶחָד, שֶׁאֵין בְּכוֹר נִפְדֶּה פָּחוּת מִבֶּן חֹדֶשׁ וְיוֹם אֶחָד, אַף כָּאן עַד שֶׁיּוֹסִיף עַל שִׁשִּׁים חֹדֶשׁ וְיוֹם אֶחָד. וּשְׁנַת חָמֵשׁ וּשְׁנַת עֶשְׂרִים יָלְפִינַן בִּגְזֵרָה שָׁוָה שָׁנָה שָׁנָה מִשְּׁנַת שִׁשִּׁים, כִּדְיָלֵיף תַּנָּא קַמָּא לְעֵיל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר:


רבי אליעזר אומר (the foregoing applies so long as they are a month and a day more than the years [which are prescribed]) - Always the fifth year and the twentieth year and the sixtieth year are like less than that age. That we derive from an analogy comparing from the usage of the word "למעלה" and "למעלה". It is stated here (Leviticus 27:7): “If the age is sixty years or over/מבן-ששים שנה ומעלה, [the equivalent is fifteen shekels in the case of a male and ten shekels for a female],” and it is stated (Numbers 18:16): “Take as their redemption price from the age of one month up/ופדויו מבן חודש ומעלה [the money equivalent of five shekels by the sanctuary weight which is twenty gerahs],” just as there it is it is one month and one day, for a firstling is not redeemed any younger than from one month and one day, so even here, until he adds on to the sixty months and one day. And the fifth year and the twentieth year we derive from an analogy – שנה שנה /a year, a year (see the Mishnah), from the sixtieth year, as the first Tanna/teacher derives above, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:1)


**הָאוֹמֵר מִשְׁקָלִי עָלָי כוּ', אִם כֶּסֶף כֶּסֶף.** אִם פֵּרֵשׁ כֶּסֶף נוֹתֵן מִשְׁקָלוֹ כֶּסֶף, וְאִם פֵּרֵשׁ זָהָב, נוֹתֵן מִשְׁקָלוֹ זָהָב. וְהָא קָמַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאִם לֹא פֵּרֵשׁ אֶלָּא אָמַר סְתָם מִשְׁקָלִי עָלָי, פָּטַר נַפְשֵׁיהּ בְּכָל דְּהוּ, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה דָבָר שֶׁכְּמוֹתוֹ רָגִיל לִהְיוֹת נִשְׁקָל בַּמָּקוֹם הַהוּא. וַאֲפִלּוּ זֶפֶת וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּצָלִים וּרְגִילִים אַנְשֵׁי הַמָּקוֹם לְמָכְרָם בְּמִשְׁקָל, וְאָמַר אֶחָד מֵאַנְשֵׁי הַמָּקוֹם הַהוּא מִשְׁקָלִי עָלָי סְתָם וְלֹא פֵּרֵשׁ, הֲרֵי זֶה נוֹתֵן מִשְׁקָלוֹ זֶפֶת אוֹ בְצָלִים וְנִפְטָר:


האומר משקלי עלי כו', אם כסף כסף – if he specified silver, he gives his weight in silver, but if he specified gold, he gives his weight in gold. But surely it comes to tell us that if he didn’t specify, but rather stated merely, “my weight is upon me,” he exempted himself from anything, as long as there will be a thing that is similar to it that is ordinarily weighed in that place. And even pitch/tar and even onions and the people of the place are accustomed to sell them by weight, and one of the people of that place said merely: “my weight is incumbent on me [as a pledge to the Temple]” but did not specify, he gives his weight in pitch/tar or in onions and is further exempt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:2)


**וּמַעֲשֶׂה בְאִמָּהּ שֶׁל יִרְמַטְיָה.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא דְּמַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מְחַסְרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, וְאִם אָדָם חָשׁוּב הוּא, אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא פֵרֵשׁ אָמְדִינַן לֵיהּ לְפִי כְבוֹדוֹ, וּמַעֲשֶׂה נַמִּי בְּאִמָּהּ שֶׁל יִרְמַטְיָה, אִשָּׁה שֶׁשְּׁמָהּ כָּךְ, שֶׁאָמְרָה מִשְׁקַל בִּתִּי עָלָי וְעָלְתָה לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, וּשְׁקָלוּהָ וְנָתְנָה מִשְׁקָלָהּ זָהָב, לְפִי שֶׁהָיְתָה אֲמוּדָה בְעֹשֶׁר:


ומעשה באמה של ירמטיה – It explains in the Gemara (Tractate Arakhin 19a) that our Mishnah is deficient and should be read as follows, and if he is a prominent person, even though he has not expressly stated, we estimate in accordance with his honor, and it happened with the mother of the Yirmatia, a woman whose name was such, that said, “I vow the weight of my daughter,” and she went up to Jerusalem and weighed her, and then she paid her weight in gold, because she was estimated in wealth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:3)


**מִשְׁקַל יָדִי עָלָי.** הֵיאַךְ הוּא שׁוֹקֵל יָדוֹ, דְּאִם רוֹצֶה מַכְבִּיד וְאִם רוֹצֶה מֵקֵל:


משקל ידי עלי – how does he weigh his hand, for if he wants he makes it heavy and if he wants, he makes it lighter?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:4)


**וּמַכְנִיסָהּ עַד מַרְפְּקוֹ.** קוד"ו בְּלַעַ"ז. מִשּׁוּם דְּבִנְדָרִים הַלֵּךְ אַחַר לְשׁוֹן בְּנֵי אָדָם, וּבְאוֹתוֹ זְמַן הָיוּ קוֹרִין יָד לַזְּרוֹעַ עַד הַמַּרְפֵּק. וּכְשֶׁמַּכְנִיס הַיָּד לְתוֹךְ חָבִית מְלֵאָה מַיִם יִשָּׁפְכוּ מִן הַמַּיִם שֶׁבֶּחָבִית כְּפִי מְקוֹם הַיָּד שֶׁהִכְנִיס בְּתוֹכָהּ עַד הַמַּרְפֵּק, וְחוֹזֵר וּמַכְנִיס בֶּחָבִית בָּשָׂר וְגִידִין וַעֲצָמוֹת שֶׁל חֲמוֹר, שֶׁמִּשְׁקָל בְּשַׂר חֲמוֹר כְּמִשְׁקַל בְּשַׂר אָדָם, וִיהֵא בְּאוֹתוֹ בָשָׂר גִּידִין וַעֲצָמוֹת כְּפִי מַה שֶּׁיֵּשׁ בַּיָּד, שֶׁאֵין מִשְׁקַל עֲצָמוֹת וְגִידִין שָׁוֶה לְמִשְׁקַל בָּשָׂר, וְנוֹתֵן לְתוֹכָהּ עַד שֶׁתַּחְזֹר וְתִתְמַלֵּא כְמוֹ שֶׁהָיְתָה, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אִיכָּא נֶפַח כְּשִׁעוּר יָדוֹ, וְשׁוֹקֵל הַבָּשָׂר וְהָעֲצָמוֹת וְהַגִּידִים הָהֵם וְהוּא מִשְׁקַל יָדוֹ:


ומכניסה עד מרפקו (pokes his hand in up to his elbow) – KUDO in the foreign tongue, because in vows one follows the language of human beings and at tht time, they would call the hand from the arm to the elbow, and when one places one’s hand into the jar filled with water, the water that is in the jar will spill out according to the place of the hand that one entered into it until the elbow (i.e., the water is displaced), and he goes back and places the flesh, sinews and bones of a donkey, for the weight of the flesh of a donkey is like the weight of the flesh of a human being, and there will be in that flesh sinews and bones according what is in the hand [of a human being] for the weight of the bones and sinews is not equivalent to the weight of the flesh, and he places into it until it returns and is filled up like it was, for now there is volume like the measurement of his hand, and he weighs the flesh and these sinews and this is the weight of his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:1:5)


**הֵיאַךְ אֶפְשָׁר לְכַוֵּן בָּשָׂר כְּנֶגֶד בָּשָׂר.** שֶׁמָּא יֵשׁ בִּבְשַׂר חֲמוֹר שֶׁנּוֹתֵן לְתוֹךְ הֶחָבִית יוֹתֵר גִּידִים וַעֲצָמוֹת מִמַּה שֶּׁיֵּשׁ בַּיָּד, אוֹ פָּחוֹת, וְנִמְצָא שֶׁאֵין הַמִּשְׁקָל מְכֻוָּן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


היאך אפשר לכוין בשר כנגד בשר – for perhaps here is in the flesh of a donkey that place that he places into the jar more sinews and bones from what is in the hand [of a human being] or less, and it is found that the weight is not equivalent. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:1)


**וְכַמָּה הוּא שָׁוֶה בְלֹא יָד.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא, שֶׁאֵין שָׁמִין אוֹתוֹ כַּמָּה יָפֶה כְשֶׁנִּקְטְעָה יָדוֹ וְכַמָּה הָיָה שָׁוֶה כְּשֶׁשְּׁתֵּי יָדָיו שְׁלֵמוֹת. שֶׁמִּי שֶׁנִּקְטְעָה יָדוֹ, כְּשֶׁבָּאִים לְשׁוּמוֹ כַּמָּה הָיָה שָׁוֶה תְחִלָּה אֵין שָׁמִין אוֹתוֹ אֶלָּא בְּזוֹל, לְפִי שֶׁרוֹאִים אוֹתוֹ מְזֻלְזָל, וְזֶה הָאוֹמֵר דְּמֵי יָדִי עָלָי הֲרֵי לֹא נִקְטְעָה יָדוֹ. לְכָךְ מְפָרֵשׁ, כַּמָּה הוּא שָׁוֶה בְלֹא יָד, כְּגוֹן אִם מְכָרוֹ רַבּוֹ כֻלּוֹ חוּץ מִיָּדוֹ, שֶׁיָּדוֹ אַחַת מֻכְתֶּבֶת לְרַבּוֹ רִאשׁוֹן, וְשָׁמִין כַּמָּה הוּא יָפֶה כְּשֶׁרַבּוֹ מוֹכֵר כֻּלּוֹ וְלֹא שִׁיֵּר מִמֶּנּוּ לְעַצְמוֹ כְּלוּם. וְכַמָּה שָׁוֶה פָּחוֹת אִם שִׁיֵּר רַבּוֹ יָדוֹ אַחַת שֶׁלֹּא מְכָרָהּ, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֵינוֹ מְזֻלְזָל, וְכָךְ הוּא נוֹתֵן לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ:


וכמה הוא שוה בלא יד – it explains in the Gemara (Tractate Arakhin 19b – see Rashi, s.v. אומד של נזקין), that we don’t valuate/estimate how beautiful when his hand is cut off and how much he would be worth when his hands are whole. For a person whose hand was cut off, when they come to valuate him how much he would be worth at first, they don’t valuate him other than cheaply, because we view him as despised, for this person who says: “the price of my hand is incumbent upon me,” his hand was not cut off. Therefore, it (i.e., the Gemara) explains how much he is worth without a hand, as for example, if his master sold all of him except for his hand, for his one hands is recorded/levied to his first master and we estimate how much he is worth when his master sells all of him and does not leave anything of him to himself. And how much less he is worth if his master left his one hand without selling it, for now he is not despised. And such he gives to the sanctity of the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:2)


**זֶה חֹמֶר בַּנְּדָרִים מִבָּעֲרָכִין.** דְּאִלּוּ אָמַר בָּעֲרָכִים עֵרֶךְ יָדִי וְרַגְלִי עָלָי, לֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן אָמַר בְּדָבָר שֶׁהַנְּשָׁמָה תְּלוּיָה בוֹ:


זה חומר בנדרם מבערכין – for whereas, if he said in valuation , that the value of my hand and/or my foot is upon me, he did not say anything, unless he said it with regard to which life depends.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:3)


**עֶרְכִּי עָלָי וָמֵת יִתְּנוּ הַיּוֹרְשִׁים.** דְּעֵרֶךְ דָּבָר קָצוּב הוּא. וְדַוְקָא כְשֶׁעָמַד בַּדִּין קֹדֶם שֶׁמֵּת, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ חַיָּב בָּעֵרֶךְ אֶלָּא אַחַר שֶׁיַּעֲמֹד לִפְנֵי הַכֹּהֵן, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְהֶעֱמִידוֹ לִפְנֵי הַכֹּהֵן. אֲבָל דָּמַי עָלָי דְּאֵינוֹ אֶלָּא מַה שֶׁיְּשׁוּמוּהוּ בְּבֵית דִּין וַהֲרֵי לֹא בָּא לִידֵי כָּךְ שֶׁהֲרֵי מֵת, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעָמַד בַּדִּין הוּא מְחֻסָּר אֻמְדָּנָא וְלֹא יִתְּנוּ הַיּוֹרְשִׁים:


ערכי עלי ומת יתנו היורשים – the value of something that is determined/clearly defined. But specifically when he stood in judgment prior to his death, because he is not liable for a valuation until after he stands before the Kohen, as it is written (Leviticus 27:8): “he shall be pesented before the priest.” But my price is incumbent upon me, which is not other than what the Jewish court will estimate him, and behold he doesn’t come to that for he has died, even though he stood in judgment, he is lacking an approximate assessment, and the heirs do not pay it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:4)


**נוֹתֵן עֵרֶךְ כֻּלּוֹ.** דְּעֵרֶךְ נְפָשׁוֹת כְּתִיב:


נותן ערך כולו – for it is written (Leviticus 27:2): “the equivalent for human being.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:2:5)


**זֶה הַכְּלָל.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי שְׁאָר אֵבָרִים שֶׁהַנְּשָׁמָה תְלוּיָה בָהֶם:


זה הכלל – to include the rest of the limbs on which life depends.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:3:1)


**חֶצְיִי עָלָי.** הָוֵי כְּעֵרֶךְ רֹאשִׁי וְעֵרֶךְ כְּבֵדִי, דַּהֲוֵי דָבָר שֶׁהַנְּשָׁמָה תְלוּיָה בוֹ:


חציי עלי – it would be like the valuation of my head and the valuation of my liver, for it is something upon which his life depends upon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:3:2)


**דְּמֵי חֶצְיִי עָלָי נוֹתֵן דְּמֵי כֻלּוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) נֶדֶר בְּעֶרְכְּךָ נְפָשׁוֹת, הִקִּישׁ נֶדֶר לְעֵרֶךְ, מַה עֵרֶךְ אָמַר עֵרֶךְ חֶצְיִי עָלָי נוֹתֵן עֵרֶךְ כֻּלּוֹ, אַף נֶדֶר אָמַר דְּמֵי חֶצְיִי עָלָי נוֹתֵן כֻּלּוֹ:


דמי חציי עלי נותן דמי כולו – as it is written (Leviticus 27:2): “explicitly vows [to the LORD] the equivalent for a human being,” an analogy is made between a vow and a valuation, just as regarding a valuation, if he said: “the valuation of half of me is incumbent on me,” he pays the whole of his valuation, so also that of a vow if he said: “the price of half of me is incumbent on me,” he pays the whole of his price.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:3:3)


**זֶה הַכְּלָל.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי כָל הָאֵבָרִים שֶׁהַנְּשָׁמָה תְלוּיָה בָהֶם, שֶׁאִם אָמַר דְּמֵי חֲצִי אוֹתוֹ אֵבָר עָלָי, כְּגוֹן שֶׁאָמַר דְּמֵי חֲצִי לִבִּי עָלָי, אוֹ דְמֵי חֲצִי כְבֵדִי עָלָי, נוֹתֵן דְּמֵי כֻלּוֹ:


זה הכלל – to include all of he limbs upon which his life depends, for if he said: “half of the price of that limb is upon me, as for example, that he said: “half of he price of my heart is upon me,” or the price of half of my liver is upon me, he gives the price of all of it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:4:1)


**מֵת הַנּוֹדֵר וְהַנִּדָּר יִתְּנוּ הַיּוֹרְשִׁים.** וְהוּא שֶׁעָמַד בַּדִּין קֹדֶם שֶׁמֵּת, כִּדְפָרֵישְׁנָא לְעֵיל. וְסֵיפָא אִצְטְרִיכָא לֵיהּ: **דָּמָיו שֶׁל פְּלוֹנִי עָלָי, מֵת הַנּוֹדֵר יִתְּנוּ הַיּוֹרְשִׁים.** דְּמַהוּ דְתֵימָא אַף עַל גַּב דְּעָמַד בַּדִּין, הוֹאִיל וּמֵת הַנּוֹדֵר קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּשׁוּמוּ הַנִּדָּר, לֹא אִשְׁתַּעְבּוּד נִכְסֵיהּ, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן אֻמְדָּנָא גִּלּוּי מִלְּתָא בְעָלְמָא הוּא, וְהוֹאִיל וְנִדָּר קַיָּם אָמְדִינַן לֵיהּ:


מת הנודר והנידר יתנו היורשים – and he that stood in judgement before he died, as was explained above, but the ending clause is necessary: “The price of so-and-so is incumbent upon me,” if the person taking the vow dies, the heirs will give the money, for you might have thought that even though that he stood in judgment, since the person making the vow died prior to making an estimate/valuation of the one whom the vow was about, his possessions are not mortgaged, for it comes to tell us that an approximate assessment/assessment by sight is a revealing a general thing, for since the person about whom the vow is taken lives, we assess him.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:5:1)


**בַּיִת זֶה קָרְבָּן.** לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת:


בית זה קרבן – for keeping the Temple in repair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:5:2)


**וְנָפַל הַבַּיִת.** קֹדֶם שֶׁהֶחְזִיק בּוֹ גִּזְבָּר:


ונפל הבית – prior to the treasurer [of the Temple] grabbed hold of him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:5:3)


**מֵת הַשּׁוֹר וְכוּ'‏ חַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּם.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן אֵין דָּמִים לַמֵּתִים אֶלָּא בְאָדָם בִּלְבַד:


מת השור וכו' חייב לשלם – and we don’t say that there are no funds for the deed, other than for a human alone.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:1)


**מְמַשְׁכְּנִין אוֹתָן.** גִּזְבָּר נִכְנָס לְבֵיתָם וְנוֹטֵל בְּעַל כָּרְחָם:


ממשכנין אותן (exact pledges from them) – the treasurer enters into their homes and takes it against their will.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:2)


**חַיָּבֵי חַטָּאוֹת וַאֲשָׁמוֹת.** כֵּיוָן דִּלְכַפָּרָה אָתוּ לֹא מַשְׁהֵי לְהוּ, וְאֵין מְמַשְׁכְּנִין אוֹתָן. וְחַטַּאת נָזִיר, הוֹאִיל וְלָאו לְכַפָּרָה אָתְיָא וְאֵינָהּ מְעַכַּבְתּוֹ מִלִּשְׁתּוֹת יַיִן, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּזְרַק הַדָּם שֶׁל אֶחָד מִן הַקָּרְבָּנוֹת הֻתַּר הַנָּזִיר לִשְׁתּוֹת יַיִן וּלְהִטָּמֵא לַמֵּתִים, זִמְנִין דְּפָשַׁע וּמַשְׁהֵי לַהּ, הִלְכָּךְ מְמַשְׁכְּנִין אוֹתוֹ עַל חַטָּאתוֹ:


חייבי חטאות ואשמות – since it is for atonement that came, they do not procrastinate [in providing the sin-offering or guilt offering] , and they do not exact pledges from them. But the sin-offering of the Nazirite, since it does not come for atonement, and does not prevent him from drinking wine, for since the blood of one of the sacrifices was sprinkled, the Nazirite is permitted to drink wine and to become defiled to the dead, on occasion that he acted negligently and delayed, therefore we exact the pledge on his sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:3)


**חַיָּבֵי עוֹלוֹת וּשְׁלָמִים.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּעוֹלָה מְכַפֶּרֶת עַל עֲשֵׂה וְעַל לָאו הַנִּתָּק לַעֲשֵׂה, הוֹאִיל וְלָאו חוֹבָה הִיא לָא חֲשִׁיב לַהּ כַּפָּרָה וְאָתֵי לְשִׁהוּיֵי, הִלְכָּךְ מְמַשְׁכְּנִים אוֹתָן. וְיֵשׁ מֵחַיָּבֵי עוֹלוֹת שֶׁאֵין מְמַשְׁכְּנִים אוֹתָן, כְּגוֹן עוֹלַת מְצֹרָע, לְפִי שֶׁהִיא מְעַכֶּבֶת טָהֳרָתוֹ, לֹא אָתֵי לְשִׁהוּיֵהּ:


חייבי עולות ושלמים – even though the burnt-offering atones for [violation of] a positive commandment and for a prohibition that after its violation is transformed into a positive commandment, since the negative commandment is not an obligation, it is not considered as an atonement and one can delay performing it, therefore, we exact a pledge from them. But there are those liable for burnt-offerings where we don’t exact a pledge from them, as for example the burnt-offering of a leper, because it delays his purification and it does not come to delay him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:4)


**כּוֹפִין אוֹתוֹ עַד שֶׁיֹּאמַר רוֹצֶה אֲנִי.** הֵיכָא דְּבֵית דִּין מְמַשְׁכְּנִים אוֹתוֹ צָרִיךְ שֶׁיֹּאמַר רוֹצֶה אֲנִי:


כופין אותו עד שיאמר רוצה אני – where that the Jewish court exacts a pledge from him, he must state: “I want to do it.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/5:6:5)


**וְכֵן אַתָּה אוֹמֵר בְּגִטֵּי נָשִׁים.** כָּל מִי שֶׁדִּינוֹ שֶׁכּוֹפִין אוֹתוֹ לְגָרֵשׁ, וְלֹא רָצָה לְגָרֵשׁ, בֵּית דִּין שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל מַכִּין אוֹתוֹ עַד שֶׁיֹּאמַר רוֹצֶה אֲנִי, וְנוֹתֵן הַגֵּט וְהוּא כָּשֵׁר:


וכן אתה אומר בגטי נשים – whomever his judgement is that they force him to divorce [his wife], the Jewish court of Israel beats him until he says: “I want [to do it] (i.e., divorce his wife), and he gives [her] the Jewish bill of divorce and it is appropriate (see also Tractate Ketubot, Chapter 7, Mishnah 10).



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:1)


**שׁוּם הַיְתוֹמִים.** בֵּית דִּין הַיּוֹרְדִים לְנִכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים לְמָכְרָן לְהַגְבּוֹת לְבַעַל חוֹב, שָׁמִין אֶת הַקַּרְקַע וּמַכְרִיזִין שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם רְצוּפִים זֶה אַחַר זֶה, כָּל הָרוֹצֶה לִקַּח יָבֹא וְיִקַּח. וְאִי בָעוּ, מַכְרִיזִין שִׁשִּׁים יוֹם כָּל שֵׁנִי וַחֲמִישִׁי, וְהָכִי עָדִיף טְפֵי. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּכִי חָשַׁבְתְּ כָּל שֵׁנִי וַחֲמִישִׁי שֶׁבְּשִׁשִּׁים יוֹם מִתְּחִלַּת יוֹם שֵׁנִי שֶׁל הַכְרָזָה רִאשׁוֹנָה לֹא מַשְׁכַּחַת לְהוּ אֶלָּא תַמְנֵי סְרֵי יוֹמֵי, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הָכִי עָדִיף, כֵּיוָן דְּמָשְׁכָא מִלְּתָא טוּבָא יָדְעֵי וְשָׁמְעֵי:


שום היתומים –([the proclamation of the sale of goods of] orphans evaluated [by the court to meet the father’s debt]) – a Jewish court that goes down into the property of orphans to sell them to cause to be collected to the creditor, we estimate the property/land and announce it thirty consecutive days one after another: “all who wish to take it should come and take.” But if they wanted, they announce for sixty days every Monday and Thursday, and this is more preferable. For even though that they considered every Monday and Thursday that are part of the sixty days from the beginning of the second day of the first public announcement, they did not find other than eighteen days; nevertheless, this is preferable since the matter drags on/stretches a lot more than they know and hear about.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:2)


**וְשׁוּם הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ.** הַמַּקְדִּישׁ שְׂדֵה מִקְנָה, שֶׁהִיא נִפְדֵּית בְּדָמִים כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִיא שָׁוָה:


ושום הקדש – a person who dedicates an acquired field [to the Temple] which is redeemed with money according to what it is worth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:3)


**בַּבֹּקֶר וּבָעֶרֶב.** בִּשְׁעַת הוֹצָאַת פּוֹעֲלִים וּבִשְׁעַת הַכְנָסַת פּוֹעֲלִים. דְּאִי אִיכָּא דְבָעֵי לְמִזְבַּן אָמַר לְהוּ לַפּוֹעֲלִים בִּשְׁעַת יְצִיאָתָן רְאוּ לִי שָׂדֶה פְּלוֹנִית אִם הִיא יָפָה, וּבִשְׁעַת הַכְנָסַת פּוֹעֲלִים כְּשֶׁשּׁוֹמֵעַ הַהַכְרָזָה נִזְכָּר וְהוֹלֵךְ וְשׁוֹאֲלָן. וּכְשֶׁהֵן מַכְרִיזִין, אוֹמְרִים, שָׂדֶה פְּלוֹנִית שֶׁכָּךְ וְכָךְ סִימָנֶיהָ, וְכָךְ וְכָךְ מְצָרֶיהָ, כָּךְ הִיא עוֹשָׂה תְּבוּאָה וְכָךְ שָׁמְאוּהָ בֵּית דִּין. וְאִם מוֹכְרִים אוֹתָהּ לִכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה, אוֹמְרִים, הַלּוֹקֵחַ יִקַּח עַל מְנָת שֶׁיִּתֵּן הַמָּעוֹת לִכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה. וְאִם מוֹכְרִים אוֹתָהּ בִּשְׁבִיל בַּעַל חוֹב, אוֹמְרִים, עַל מְנָת שֶׁיִּתֵּן הַמָּעוֹת לְבַעַל חוֹב. מִשּׁוּם דְּאִיכָּא לוֹקֵחַ דְּנִיחָא לֵיהּ לִקַּח כְּדֵי לִפְרֹעַ לְאִשָּׁה דְנַסְבָּא פּוּרְתָּא פּוּרְתָּא, וְאִיכָּא דְנִיחָא לֵיהּ טְפֵי לִפְרַע לְבַעַל חוֹב שֶׁנּוֹטֵל זוּזִים חֲסֵרִים וּשְׁבוּרִים, שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ סוֹחֲרִים לְהַקְפִּיד בְּכָךְ. אַף עַל גַּב דְּקַיְמָא לָן דְּאֵין בֵּית דִּין נִזְקָקִים לְנִכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים לְמָכְרָן, וְהָכָא תְנַן שׁוּם הַיְתוֹמִים וְכוּ'‏ דְּמַשְׁמַע שֶׁבֵּית דִּין מוֹכְרִים נִכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים, הָא אָמְרִינַן דְּעַל שְׁלֹשָׁה דְּבָרִים בִּלְבַד בֵּית דִּין נִזְקָקִים לִמְכֹּר בְּנִכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים, עַל בַּעַל חוֹב נָכְרִי שֶׁהָיָה אֲבִיהֶם חַיָּב לוֹ וְהָיְתָה רִבִּית אוֹכֶלֶת בָּהֶן, וְעַל כְּתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה מִשּׁוּם דְּאִיכָּא רַוְחָא לַיְתוֹמִים כְּשֶׁתִּגְבֶּה כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, דְּכָל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא גָּבְתָה כְּתֻבָּתָהּ יֵשׁ לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹת מִן הַיְתוֹמִים, גָּבְתָה כְּתֻבָּתָהּ שׁוּב אֵין לָהּ מְזוֹנוֹת. וְעַל חוֹב שֶׁהוֹדָה אֲבִיהֶן בִּשְׁעַת מִיתָתוֹ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לִפְלוֹנִי אֶצְלוֹ מָנֶה, אוֹ שֶׁנִּדּוּהוּ בֵּית דִּין עַל חוֹב זֶה וּמֵת בְּנִדּוּיוֹ, אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ עֲדַיִן זְמַן פְּרִיעַת הַחוֹב כְּשֶׁמֵּת, דְּלָא עֲבִיד אֱינַשׁ דְּפָרַע בְּגוֹ זִמְנֵיהּ. וּכְשֶׁבֵּית דִּין נִזְקָקִים לְנִכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים עַל שְׁלֹשָׁה דְבָרִים הַלָּלוּ, מַעֲמִידִים לָהֶם אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס וּמוֹכְרִים בְּהַכְרָזָה, כְּדִתְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי בְקַרְקָעוֹת. אֲבָל בְּמִטַּלְטְלִין, הַשְׁתָּא דְקַיְמָא לָן מִטַּלְטְלֵי דְיַתְמֵי מִשְׁתַּעְבְּדֵי לְבַעַל חוֹב, הֲרֵי בַּעַל חוֹב נִשְׁבָּע וְגוֹבֶה חוֹבוֹ מִן הַמִּטַּלְטְלִין וְאֵין צְרִיכִין הַכְרָזָה, שֶׁאֵין מַכְרִיזִים לֹא עַל הָעֲבָדִים וְלֹא עַל הַשְּׁטָרוֹת וְלֹא עַל הַמִּטַּלְטְלִין:


בבוקר ובערב – at the time when the workers go out [to work] and at the time when they go home. For if there those who desire to sell it, he tells the workers at the time they go out [to work]: “See for me a particular field if it is nice,” and at the time when they go home, when he hears the public announcement, he recalls and goes and asks them. But when they make the announcement, they say: “This particular field – that such-and-such are its signs/marks [of identification], such and such are its pathways, such is how it makes grain and such the Jewish court has provided an estimate of it. And if they sell it for the [payment of] the Ketubah of his wife, they state: “The purchaser will buy it on the condition that he (i.e., the seller) will give the monies for the Ketubah of his wife.” But if they sell it for the creditor, they state: “On the condition that he gives the monies to the creditor.” Because there is a purchaser that it is pleasant for him to purchase it in order to pay the woman that he married bit by bit, and there is a case where it is more pleasant for him to pay the creditor who takes defective and broken Zuzim (i.e., coins), for it is not the manner of merchants to be so exacting in this. But even though we hold that the Jewish court does not require the property of orphans to sell them, and here (i.e., in this Mishnah) it is taught that “[the proclamation of the sale of goods of] orphans evaluated [by the court to meet the father’s debt] is thirty days,” that implies that the Jewish court does sell the property of orphans. But we state that only for three things does the Jewish court need to sell the property of orphans: a heathen creditor that their father owed him, and that interest [payments] eat up [their settlement], and on the Ketubah of a wife because there is ample provision for the orphans when she collects her Ketubah/marital settlement, for all the while that she didn’t collect her Ketubah settlement she has provisions/alimentation from the orphans; once she collects her Ketubah settlement, she no longer has alimentation. And on a debt that their father admitted to/acknowledged at the time of his death that someone has [a claim] of a Maneh, or that the Jewish court excommunicated him on this debt and he died in his being excommunicated, or that the time had not yet arrived at the time of his death for repayment of the debt, for a person does not act in the midst of its time, and when the Jewish court needs the property of the orphans for these things, they appoint for them an administrator (or guardian) and they sell it through public announcement, as is taught in our Mishnah. But these words apply with regard to land, but regarding movables, now that we hold that movables of orphans can be mortgaged to a creditor, the creditor needs to take an oath and collect his lien from the movables and there is no need for public announcement, for we don’t make public announcements on either slaves nor on documents nor on movable properties.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:4)


**יַדִּירֶנָּה הֲנָאָה.** עַל דַּעַת רַבִּים, נֶדֶר שֶׁאֵין לוֹ הַתָּרָה, שֶׁלֹּא יַחֲזִירֶנָּה לְעוֹלָם. דְּשֶׁמָּא בְעָרְמָה מְגָרֵשׁ לָהּ כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּגְבֶּה כְּתֻבָּתָהּ מִן הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ:


ידירנה הנאה – with the knowledge of the many a vow that has no releasing/loosening [from the vow], so that he will never return her [as his wife] ever, for perhaps through a subtlety, he divorces her in order that she can collect her Ketubah settlement from that which was dedicated to the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:5)


**קְנוּנְיָא.** עָרְמָה:


קנוניא (a conspiracy to defraud and divide the profits) – a subtlety.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ.** לֹא פְלִיגֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ הָכָא אִם אָדָם עוֹשֶׂה קְנוּנְיָא עַל הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ אִי לֹא, דְּבִשְׁכִיב מֵרַע תַּרְוַיְהוּ מוֹדוּ דִבְוַדַּאי לְעוֹלָם אֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה קְנוּנְיָא עַל הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ, דְּאֵין אָדָם חוֹטֵא וְלֹא לוֹ. וּבְבָרִיא תַּרְוַיְהוּ מוֹדוּ דְּעוֹשֶׂה קְנוּנְיָא עַל הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ. כִּי פְלִיגֵי, בִּשְׁאֵלָה, רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר סָבַר אֵין נִשְׁאָלִין עַל הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ, אֲפִלּוּ אוֹמֵר לוֹ לֶחָכָם לֹא לְדַעַת כֵּן נָדַרְתִּי וְהֶקְדֵּשׁ טָעוּת הוּא, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אֵין חָכָם מַתִּיר נִדְרוֹ, דְּסָבַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר הֶקְדֵּשׁ בְּטָעוּת הָוֵי הֶקְדֵּשׁ, וּלְפִי שֶׁהַמַּקְדִּישׁ הַזֶּה אֵינוֹ מוֹצֵא חָכָם שֶׁיַּתִּירֶנּוּ לְכָךְ עוֹשֶׂה קְנוּנְיָא זוֹ. וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ סָבַר נִשְׁאָלִין עַל הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ, וְכִי אָמַר לֹא לְדַעַת כֵּן נָדַרְתִּי וְטָעִיתִי, מַתִּירִין לוֹ, דְּהֶקְדֵּשׁ שֶׁבְּטָעוּת לֹא הָוֵי הֶקְדֵּשׁ. הִלְכָּךְ אֵין צָרִיךְ לִדּוֹר הֲנָאָה, דְּאִי מִשּׁוּם קְנוּנְיָא עֲבִיד, הֲוָה מִתְּשִׁיל עַל נִדְרֵיהּ וְלֹא הָיָה צָרִיךְ לְגָרְשָׁהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


ר' יהושע אומר אינו צריך – Rabbi Eliezer and Rabbi Yehoshua do not disagree here if a person performs a conspiracy on something dedicated to the Temple to defraud and divide the profits or not, both of them admit regarding a person that is on his deathbed, that always, he would not perform a conspiracy on something dedicated to the Temple, for a person does not sin regarding something that is not his. But regarding someone healthy, they both admit that he does make a conspiracy on something dedicated to the Temple. What they do disagree on is on a question [to a Sage] of dedication of something to the Temple for Rabbi Eliezer holds that they do not appear before a Sage regarding a question of dedicating something to the Temple, and even if he states to the Sage that it was not for this purpose that he vowed and the dedication to the Temple is in error, even so, the Sage cannot release his vow, for Rabbi Eliezer holds that a dedication done to the Temple in error is a valid dedication, and because this person making a dedication cannot find a Sage who will release him, he performs this conspiracy. But Rabbi Yehoshua holds that they do appear before a Sage regarding someone who dedicates something to the Temple [who wants to be released] for he states that it was not for that purpose that I made the vow and I errored, they release him, for something dedicated to the Temple in error is not a dedication to the Temple. Therefore, he does not have to take a vow [against] deriving any benefit, for if it was for making a conspiracy that he did it, he would come [before a Sage] to be absolved of his vow and he would not have to divorce her. And he Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:1:7)


**הֶעָרֵב לָאִשָּׁה בִּכְתֻבָּתָהּ.** וְאֵין לַבַּעַל נְכָסִים, וְצָרִיךְ הֶעָרֵב לִפְרֹעַ כְּתֻבָּתָהּ. לֹא יִפְרַע הֶעָרֵב הַכְּתֻבָּה עַד שֶׁיַּדִּירֶנָּה הַבַּעַל תְּחִלָּה שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל לְהַחֲזִיר אוֹתָהּ. דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא דַעְתּוֹ לְהַחְזִירָהּ וְלֶאֱכֹל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ לְאַחַר שֶׁתִּגְבֶּה מִן הֶעָרֵב. וּפְסַק הַהֲלָכָה בְּדִין הֶעָרֵב בִּכְתֻבָּה מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסוֹף בָּבָא בַּתְרָא:


הערב לאשה בכתובתה – and the husband lacks property and needs a guarantor to pay off her Ketubah settlement. The guarantor should not pay her Ketubah settlement until the husband first makes her take a vow that he will not be able to take her back [as his wife]. For we suspect lest it is his intention to take her back and to consume her Ketubah settlement after she collects it from the guarantor. And the legal decision in the law of the guarantor of the Ketubah is explained at the end of [Tractate] Bava Batra [Chapter 10, Mishnah 7].



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:2:1)


**וְהָיְתָה עָלָיו כְּתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁקָּדְמוּ גֵרוּשִׁין לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ, דְּעַכְשָׁיו לֵיכָּא קְנוּנְיָא:


והיתה עליו כתובת אשה – as for example that the divorce of his wife preceded the dedication of property to the Temple, for now there is no conspiracy to defraud and divide the profits (see Tractate Arakhin 23a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:2:2)


**אֶלָּא הַפּוֹדֶה פוֹדֶה.** בַּעֲלָהּ פּוֹדֶה אוֹתָן מִן הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ בְּזוֹל בְּדָבָר מֻעָט כְּדֵי לִפְרֹעַ לָאִשָּׁה כְתֻבָּתָהּ, דְּוַדַּאי לֹא חָיֵל עָלַיְהוּ הֶקְדֵּשׁ שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינָן שֶׁלּוֹ. וְהַאי דָּבָר מֻעָט, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יֹאמְרוּ הֶקְדֵּשׁ יוֹצֵא לְחֻלִּין בְּלֹא פִּדְיוֹן:


אלא הפודה פודה – her husband redeems them from the property of the Temple cheaply for a small amount in order to pay the woman her Ketubah settlement, for certainly the dedication to the Temple does not take effect on them, for they are not his. And this is a small amount, as a decree, lest they say that what is dedicated to the Temple goes out to become unconsecrated without redemption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:2:3)


**הִקְדִּישׁ תִּשְׁעִים וְהָיָה חוֹבוֹ מֵאָה.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּחוֹבוֹ יוֹתֵר עַל הֶקְדֵּשׁוֹ, לֹא אָמְרִינַן הַאי לָאו אַדַּעְתָּא דְהָנֵי נִכְסֵיהּ אוֹזְפֵיהּ אֶלָּא הֵימוֹנֵי הֵימְנֵיהּ הִלְכָּךְ לֹא גָבֵי מֵהֶקְדֵּשׁ, אֶלָּא אָמְרִינַן אַדַּעְתָּא דְהָנֵי נִכְסֵי אוֹזְפֵיהּ, וְגָבֵי מִנַּיְהוּ. וְעַד כַּמָּה, עַד פַּלְגָּא. אֲבָל אִם הַנְּכָסִים שֶׁהִקְדִּישׁ אֵין שָׁוִין חֲצִי הַחוֹב, לֹא גָבֵי מִנַּיְהוּ, דְּוַדַּאי לָאו אַדַּעְתָּא דְהָנֵי נִכְסֵי אוֹזְפֵיהּ, דְּאֵין אָדָם עָשׂוּי לִקְנוֹת קַרְקַע בְּיוֹתֵר מִפִּי שְׁנַיִם מִמַּה שֶּׁהִיא שָׁוָה:


הקדיש תשעים והיה חובה מאה – even though his liability is larger than that which he dedicated to the Temple, we don’t say that it was not the intention that these possessions would be borrowed but rather he is completely believed therefore he did not collect that which was dedicated to the Temple. But we state that it was the intention that these possessions were borrowed, and he collected from them. And up to how much? Up to one half. But if the possessions that he dedicated to the Temple are not worth half of the liability, he does not collect from them, for it was not with the intention of these properties that he borrowed, for a person is not used to purchasing land for more than double than it is worth.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:1)


**מְמַשְׁכְּנִין אוֹתָן.** גִּזְבָּר נִכְנָס לְבָתֵּיהֶם וְנוֹטֵל בְּעַל כָּרְחָן:


ממשכנין אותן (as a follow up to Tractate Arakhin, Chapter 5, Mishnah 6) – a treasurer enters into their homes and takes [the surety/pledge] against their will.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:2)


**מָזוֹן וּכְסוּת וּמִטָּה סַנְדָּלִים וּתְפִלִּין.** עַל כֻּלָּן מְשַׁיְּרִים לוֹ מָעוֹת לִקְנוֹתָן אִם אֵין לוֹ. דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְאִם מָךְ הוּא מֵעֶרְכְּךָ, וְדָרְשֵׁי רַבָּנָן לִקְרָא הָכִי, וְאִם מָךְ, תַּעֲשֶׂה שֶׁיִּשָּׁאֵר הוּא, שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לוֹ הֲוָיָה וְחִיּוּת, מֵעֶרְכְּךָ, מִדְּמֵי הָעֵרֶךְ. וּמַשְׁמַע הוּא יֵשׁ לוֹ הֲוָיָה וְחִיּוּת מִדְּמֵי הָעֵרֶךְ, אֲבָל לֹא לְאִשְׁתּוֹ וְלֹא לְבָנָיו:


מזון וכסו ומטה סנדלים ותפילין – on all of hem they leave over for him money to purchase them if he lacks them, as it is written (Leviticus 27:8): “But if one cannot afford the equivalent, [he shall be presented before the priest, and the priest shall assess him,” and the Rabbis expound upon this Biblical verse thusly: “but if one cannot afford/ואם מך “ – it will be made that he will remain, that he would have existence/stability (הויה ) and support/livelihood (חיות ), “the equivalent/מערכך “- from the money of the valuation. And this implies that he has existence and livelihood from the money of the valuation, but not for his wife and not for his children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:3)


**מִכָּל מִין וָמִין.** מִכָּל אֻמָּנוּת שֶׁצְּרִיכָה אַרְבָּעָה וַחֲמִשָּׁה כֵּלִים:


מכל מין ומין – from all of the trades/skilled labors that require four or five utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:4)


**מַעֲצָדִים.** דולדור"א בְּלַעַ"ז. שֶׁמַּחְלִיקִים בָּהּ פְּנֵי הַלּוּחַ:


מעצדים (adze) -DULDORA in the foreign tongue, that smoothens/levels the face of the board/tablet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:5)


**מְגֵרָה.** כְּעֵין סַכִּין אָרֹךְ מָלֵא פְּגִימוֹת. וּלְשׁוֹן מִקְרָא, מַשּׂוֹר, סיג"א בְּלַעַ"ז:


מגירה (saw/plane) – a kind of long knife filled with notches, And the language is Biblical,משור/saw (see Isaiah 10:15: “[Does an ax boast over him who hews with it] or a saw magnify itself above him who wields it/אם-יתגדל המשור על מניפו ,” SIGA in the foreign tongue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:3:6)


**צִמְדּוֹ.** צֶמֶד בָּקָר. שֶׁאֵלּוּ הֵן כְּלֵי אֻמָּנוּתוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, דְּצֶמֶד בָּקָר וַחֲמוֹר נְכָסִים נִינְהוּ, וְלָא חֲשִׁיבֵי כְּלֵי אֻמָּנוּת:


צמדו – the yoke of cattle. For they are the utensils of his trade/craft, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer that the yoke of cattle and a donkey are property, and are not considered utensils of a craft/trade.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:4:1)


**מִין אֶחָד מְרֻבֶּה וּמִין אֶחָד מֻעָט.** כְּגוֹן שְׁלֹשָׁה מַעֲצָדִים וּמְגֵרָה אַחַת:


מין אחד מרובה ומין אחד מועט – as for example, three adzes and one saw.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:4:2)


**נוֹתְנִים לוֹ שְׁנַיִם מִן הַמְרֻבֶּה.** וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁי נוֹטֵל הַגִּזְבָּר:


נותנים לו שנים מן המרובה – and the third the treasurer takes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:4:3)


**וְכֹל שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ מִן הַמֻּעָט.** מַנִּיחִים לוֹ, וְלֹא זָבְנִינַן לֵיהּ אַחֲרִיתִי. דְּכִי הֵיכִי דְּעַד הַשְׁתָּא סַגִּי לֵיהּ בְּהָכִי, הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי תִסְגֵּי לֵיהּ. דְּמַהוּ דְּתֵימָא עַד הַשְׁתָּא הָווּ אֱינָשֵׁי מוֹשְׁלֵי לֵיהּ, מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ מִין אֶחָד מְרֻבֶּה לְאוֹשׁוֹלֵי לְאַחֲרִינֵי, הַשְׁתָּא שֶׁהַגִּזְבָּר לָקַח הַמְרֻבֶּה לֹא מַשְׁכַּח מַאן דְּמוֹשִׁיל לֵיהּ, הִלְכָּךְ נְזָבֵין לֵיהּ אַחֲרִיתִי, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


וכל שיש לו מן המועט – they leave for him and we don’t sell him another. For in order that it is sufficient for him until now with this, now also it will be sufficient for him. For you might have thought that until now there were people that would lend him, because he had one kind of utensil to lend him another, now that the treasurer took from the abundant [tools], there is no found who would lend him [another], therefore, we will sell him another, it comes to tell us that this is incorrect (see Talmud Arakhin 24a).



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:1)


**אֵין לוֹ בִכְסוּת אִשְׁתּוֹ וּבָנָיו.** שֶׁאֵין אֵלּוּ נְכָסִים שֶׁלּוֹ:


אין לו בכסות אשתו ובניו – because these properties are not his.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:2)


**שֶׁצְּבָעָן לִשְׁמָן.** לְשֵׁם אִשְׁתּוֹ וּבָנָיו:


שצבען לשמן – for the sake of his wife and children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:3)


**וְלֹא בְסַנְדָּלִים חֲדָשִׁים.** רְבוּתָא אַשְׁמוֹעִינַן דְּאַף עַל גַּב דַּעֲדַיִן לֹא נָעֲלוּ אוֹתָן הֲרֵי הֵן בְּחֶזְקָתָן מִשְּׁעַת לְקִיחָה:


לא בסנדלים חדשים – it comes to teach us something remarkable for even though that they still had not worn them (i.e., the shoes), they are he time of their purchasing/acquiring them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:4)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאָמְרוּ עֲבָדִים נִמְכָּרִים בִּכְסוּתָן לְשֶׁבַח.** כְּסוּת יָפָה שֶׁלָּהֶן מְשַׁבְּחָם וּמַעֲלֶה אֶת דְּמֵיהֶן. כְּגוֹן גַּבֵּי נִכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים שֶׁאִם תִּלָּקַח לְעֶבֶד כְּסוּת בִּשְׁלֹשִׁים דִּינָר הוּא מַשְׁבִּיחַ מָנֶה עַל דָּמִים שֶׁשָּׁוֶה עַכְשָׁיו:


אע"פ שאמרו עבדים נמכרים בכסותן לשבח – their nice raiment/clothing praises and raises their monetary value, as for example regarding the possessions of orphans, that if clothing worth thirty Denar should be acquired by a slave, he should ameliorate it by a Maneh on the monetary value that it is worth currently.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:5)


**לָאִטְלִיס.** לְיוֹם הַשּׁוּק:


לאטליס – to the market day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:6)


**לַכְּרָךְ.** שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ סוֹחֲרִים לָבֹא שָׁם וְקוֹנִים הַמַּרְגָּלִיּוֹת בְּיֹקֶר:


לכרך – that it is the manner of merchants to come there and purchase pearls at an expensive price.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:7)


**אֵין לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ אֶלָּא מְקוֹמוֹ.** בְּמַרְגָּלִית:


אין להקדש אלא מקומו – regarding a pearl.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 6:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/6:5:8)


**וּשְׁעָתוֹ.** בְּעֶבֶד, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְנָתַן אֶת הָעֶרְכְּךָ בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא, שֶׁלֹּא יַשְׁהֶה. קֹדֶשׁ לַה', מַשְׁמַע כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא קֹדֶשׁ לַה'‏ כְּגוֹן סְתָם הֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת שֶׁהֵן לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת, כֻּלָּן יִהְיוּ נוֹתְנִים בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא מִיָּד, שֶׁלֹּא יַשְׁהֶה אוֹתָן. וְטַעֲמָא כָּתַב הָרַמְבַּ"ם דְּזִמְנִין דְּאָתוּ לְאַשְׁהוֹיִנְהוּ כְּדֵי לְהַשְׁבִּיחַ וְאָתוּ לִידֵי פְּסֵידָא, וּמֵהַאי טַעֲמָא נַמִּי אֵין מִשְׂתַּכְּרִים בְּשֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ:


ושעתו – regarding a slave, as it is written (Leviticus 27:23): “and he shall pay the assessment as of that day,” that he not delay it. “a sacred donation to the LORD” (ibid.), implying that everything that is a sacred donation to the LORD, such as mere donations to the Sanctuary, which are for the repair of the Temple, all of them should be given on that day immediately, so that he would not delay them. And the reason is, as Maimonides wrote (in his commentary to the Mishnah), that sometimes when they come delay [their sale] in order to increase their value, but [in actuality] they come to a loss in value, and for this reason, also, one does not profit from that which is dedicated to the Temple.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:1)


**אֵין מַקְדִּישִׁין.** שְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה:


אין מקדישין – an ancestral field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:2)


**פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁתֵּי שָׁנִים.** דִּכְתִיב בְּמַקְדִּישׁ שְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה (ויקרא כז) עַל פִּי הַשָּׁנִים הַנּוֹתָרוֹת, וְאֵין שָׁנִים פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁתַּיִם. הַמַּקְדִּישׁ שְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה בְּשָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁל יוֹבֵל וּבָא לְגָאֲלָהּ, נוֹתֵן בְּבֵית כּוֹר חֲמִשִּׁים סֶלַע, כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) זֶרַע חֹמֶר שְׂעֹרִים וְגוֹ'. וְאִם הִקְדִּישָׁהּ עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים אוֹ עֶשְׂרִים אַחַר הַיּוֹבֵל, מְחַשְּׁבִים הַשָּׁנִים הַנּוֹתָרוֹת עַד יוֹבֵל הַבָּא, וְנוֹתֵן סֶלַע וּפֻנְדְיוֹן לְכָל שָׁנָה, כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) וְאִם אַחַר הַיּוֹבֵל יַקְדִּישׁ שָׂדֵהוּ וְחִשַּׁב לוֹ וְגוֹ'‏ וְנִגְרַע מֵעֶרְכֶּךָ, וְהַיְנוּ גֵרוּעַ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן חֲמִשִּׁים שְׁלֵמִים. וְהַמַּקְדִּישׁ שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי הַיּוֹבֵל, פּוֹדֶה אוֹתָהּ בִּשְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים וּשְׁנֵי פֻנְדְיוֹנִים, כְּדִכְתִיב עַל פִּי הַשָּׁנִים הַנּוֹתָרוֹת, וְכָךְ הַחֶשְׁבּוֹן עוֹלֶה שֶׁל גְּאֻלַּת שְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה לְכָל שָׁנָה סֶלַע וּפֻנְדְיוֹן, שֶׁהֲרֵי אִם הִקְדִּישָׁהּ בְּשָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁל אַחַר הַיּוֹבֵל הָיָה פוֹדֶה בַּחֲמִשִּׁים שֶׁקֶל, צֵא מֵהֶן לְאַרְבָּעִים וְתֵשַׁע שָׁנִים הַבָּאִים עַד יוֹבֵל הַבָּא אַרְבָּעִים וְתֵשַׁע סְלָעִים, נִמְצָא סֶלַע לְכָל שָׁנָה וְסֶלַע יוֹתֵר, וְזֶה הַסֶּלַע הַיּוֹתֵר חִלְּקוּהוּ לְפֻנְדְיוֹנִים, שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּכָל סֶלַע אַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁמֹנָה פֻּנְדְיוֹנִין, הֲרֵי סֶלַע וּפֻנְדְיוֹן לְשָׁנָה פָּחוֹת פֻּנְדְיוֹן. וּמַה שֶׁהִצְרִיכוּהוּ לִתֵּן סֶלַע וּפֻנְדְיוֹן לְשָׁנָה, לְמ"ט שָׁנִים מ"ט סְלָעִים וּמ"ט פֻּנְדְיוֹנִין וְיֵשׁ כָּאן פֻּנְדְיוֹן אֶחָד יוֹתֵר עַל חֲמִשִּׁים שֶׁקֶל שֶׁאָמְרָה תוֹרָה, אוֹתוֹ הַפֻּנְדְיוֹן הוּא קַלְבּוֹן לִפְרוֹטְרוֹט, כְּלוֹמַר בִּשְׁבִיל הַהֶכְרֵעַ שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִתֵּן לְכָל שֶׁקֶל וְשֶׁקֶל בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ. אִי נַמִּי, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַסֶּלַע אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא אַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁמֹנָה פֻּנְדְיוֹנוֹת, חַיָּב לִתֵּן אַרְבָּעִים וְתִשְׁעָה, דְּהַיְנוּ סֶלַע וּפֻנְדְיוֹן לְאַרְבָּעִים וְתֵשַׁע שָׁנָה, לְפִי שֶׁהַנּוֹתֵן פֻּנְדְיוֹנִים לִקַּח סֶלַע מִן הַשֻּׁלְחָנִי נוֹתֵן אַרְבָּעִים וְתִשְׁעָה פֻּנְדְיוֹנִים, וְהַיְנוּ קַלְבּוֹן לִפְרוֹטְרוֹט. וְהַמַּקְדִּישׁ שְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁתֵּי שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי הַיּוֹבֵל אֵין כָּאן גֵּרוּעַ, וְאִם בָּא לְגָאֲלָהּ נוֹתֵן חֲמִשִּׁים שְׁלֵמִים, דְּגַבֵּי גֵרוּעַ כְּתִיב עַל פִּי הַשָּׁנִים הַנּוֹתָרוֹת וְנִגְרַע, וּמִעוּט שָׁנִים שְׁנַיִם. וְעֵצָה טוֹבָה קָמַשְׁמַע לָן מַתְנִיתִין שֶׁיְּהֵא אָדָם חָס עַל נְכָסָיו וְלֹא [יַקְדִּישׁ] פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁתֵּי שָׁנִים, שֶׁלֹּא יַפְסִיד אַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁמֹנָה סְלָעִים:


פחות משתי שנים (less than two years before the Jubilee year) – as it is written, regarding one who dedicates an ancestral field (Leviticus 27:18): “[the priest shall compute the price] according to the years that are left [until the jubilee year],” and [the word] שנים/years is not les than two. A person who dedicates his ancestral field in the first year of the jubilee and comes to redeem it, gives fifty Sela for an area requiring a KOR of seed, as it is written (Leviticus 27:16): “fifty shekels of silver to a HOMER of barley seed, etc.” But if he dedicated it ten or twenty years after the Jubilee year, we calculate the remaining years until the coming Jubilee year, and he gives a SELAH and a PUNDIYON for each year, as it is written (Leviticus 27:18): “But if he consecrates his land after the jubilee, the priest shall compute the price according to the years that are left [until the jubilee year], and its assessment shall be reduced,” that is, the deduction [from the price of redemption according to the years of redemption] that he doesn’t give fifty complete [years]. But a person who dedicates [his field] two years before the Jubilee [year], he redeems it for two SELA and two PUNDIYONIM, as it is written (Leviticus 27:18): “according to the years that are left”, and such is the accountability which goes up of the redemption of the ancestral field for each year a SELA and a PUNDIYO, for if he dedicated it in the first year after the Jubilee he would redeem it for fifty shekel, deduct from them the forty-nine coming years until the next Jubilee forty-nine shekel, it is found a Sela for each year and an additional Sela, and this additional Sela is divided into PUNDIYONIM, for in each SELA there are forty- eight PUNDIYONIM, therefore a SELA and a PUNDIYON for each year less a PUNDIYON. And just as they required him to give a SELA and a PUNDIYON for each year, for forty-nine years – forty-nine SELA and forty-nine PUNDIYONIM, and there is here one additional PUNDIYON more than the fifty shekel that the Torah mentioned, that PUNDIYON is agio (an addition) to the units (making fifty, as a round sum, instead of forty-eight – see Tractate Bekhorot 50a), meaning to say for the customary addition weight in retailing that he must give for each and every shekel on its own. Alternatively, even though the Selah is not other than forty-eight Pundiyonot, he is required to give forty nine, which is a Selah and a Pundiyon for forty-nine years, because one who gives Pundiyonim to purchase a Sela from the money-changer gives forty- nine Pundiyonim, and that is an agio (an addition) to the units (making fifty, as a round sum, instead of forty-eight. And the person who dedicates his ancestral field less than two years prior to the Jubilee year, there isn’t deduction from the price of redemption according to the years of possession (see Tractate Arakhin 24a), but if he comes to redeem it, he gives fifty complete [shekels], for concerning deduction, it is written (Leviticus 27:18): “according to the years that are left until the jubilee, and its assessment shall be so reduced,” and the minimum of שנים/years is two years. And it is good advice that our Mishnah teaches us that a person should have consideration for his possessions (see Tractate Negaim, Chapter 12, Mishnah 5) and not dedicate them [to the Temple] less than two years [from the Jubilee year] and not lose forty-eight Sela.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:3)


**וְלֹא גוֹאֲלִין אַחַר הַיּוֹבֵל.** לָאו אַחַר הַיּוֹבֵל סָמוּךְ לַיּוֹבֵל קָאָמַר, אֶלָּא הַאי אַחַר אֲפִלּוּ אַחַר עֶשְׂרִים אוֹ שְׁלֹשִׁים שָׁנָה קָאֵי. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, הַבָּא לִגְאֹל בְּאֶמְצַע הַיּוֹבֵל וְלִתֵּן סֶלַע וּפֻנְדְיוֹן לְשָׁנִים הַבָּאִים, וְהָיָה עוֹמֵד בְּנִיסָן שֶׁהוּא אֶמְצַע שָׁנָה, לֹא יֹאמַר חֲצִי סֶלַע וַחֲצִי פֻנְדְיוֹן אֲנִי נוֹתֵן מִשָּׁנָה זוֹ, אֶלָּא נוֹתֵן סֶלַע וּפֻנְדְיוֹן שָׁלֵם. וְהַיְנוּ דְּקָאָמַר אֵין גּוֹאֲלִין פָּחוֹת מִשָּׁנָה, דְּכַמָּה דְלָא נַפְקָא לַהּ כֻּלַּהּ שַׁתָּא לֹא מִגָּרְעָא לַהּ כְּלָל וְלֹא יָהֵיב גְּאֻלַּת חֲצִי שָׁנָה אֶלָּא גְאֻלַּת שָׁנָה שְׁלֵמָה. וּמַה טַּעַם, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין מְחַשְּׁבִים חֳדָשִׁים עִם הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ. הַבָּא לִגְאֹל שָׂדֵהוּ חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים וָחֵצִי לִפְנֵי הַיּוֹבֵל, אֵין מְחַשְּׁבִין אוֹתָן שִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים שֶׁיָּצְאוּ מִשָּׁנָה שִׁשִּׁית, אֶלָּא נוֹתֵן שֵׁשׁ סְלָעִים וְשִׁשָּׁה פֻנְדְיוֹנִים, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְחִשַּׁב לוֹ הַכֹּהֵן אֶת הַכֶּסֶף עַל פִּי הַשָּׁנִים הַנּוֹתָרוֹת, שָׁנִים אַתָּה מְחַשֵּׁב וְאִי אַתָּה מְחַשֵּׁב חֳדָשִׁים, כְּלוֹמַר כָּל מַה שֶׁנּוֹתַר חִשּׁוּב שָׁנִים, לַעֲשׂוֹת חֹדֶשׁ הַנּוֹתַר שָׁנָה, וְאִי אַתָּה מְחַשֵּׁב הֶחֳדָשִׁים שֶׁיָּצְאוּ, לִגְרֹעַ מִן הַשֶּׁקֶל וּפֻנְדְיוֹן בִּשְׁבִילָם:


ולא גואלין אחר היובל – and not after the Jubilee, it is stating near the Jubilee [year], but this “after” refers to even after twenty or thirty years. And this is what it says: A person who comes to redeem [his field] in the middle of the Jubilee and to give a Sela and a Pundiyon for the coming years, and he would stand in Nisan, which is the middle of the year, he should not say: “I give half-a-Sela and half-a-Pundiyon from this year, but rather, he gives a complete Sela and Pundiyon. And that is why it (i.e., the Mishnah) states, “They do not redeem it less than a year after the Jubilee”, for as long as the entire year did not go out, he does not deduct anything at all and he does give a redemption fee for half-a year- but rather a full year’s redemption fee. And what is the reason? Because we don’t calculate months with the Dedication to the Temple. A person who comes to redeem his field five and one-half years before the Jubilee [year], we don’t calculate them as six months that had passed from the sixth year, but rather, he gives six Sela and six Pundiyonim, as it is written (Leviticus 27:18): “the priest shall compute the price according to the years that are left,” years – you calculate, but you don’t calculate months, meaning to say, all that which remains is considered years, to make remaining month a year, but you don not calculate the months that passed, to deduct from the Shekel and Pundiyon for them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:4)


**אֲבָל הֶקְדֵּשׁ מְחַשֵּׁב חֳדָשִׁים.** כְּלוֹמַר, אִם רֶוַח שֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ הוּא שֶׁנַּחְשֹׁב אוֹתָהּ יְצִיאַת חֲצִי שָׁנָה שֶׁיָּצְאָה, כְּגוֹן דִּמְקַדְּשָׁהּ בְּפַלְגָא דְאַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁמֹנָה לַיּוֹבֵל, דְּאִי חָשְׁבַת לְהַהוּא יְצִיאָה הָוֵי לֵיהּ פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁתֵּי שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי הַיּוֹבֵל וְלֹא מִפַּרְקָא בְגֵרוּעַ אֶלָּא בַחֲמִשִּׁים, וְאִי אָמַרְתְּ דְּאֵין מְחַשְּׁבִים אוֹתָן חֳדָשִׁים שֶׁיָּצְאוּ, הֲוָה הָכָא שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי הַיּוֹבֵל, וְאִיכָּא פְסֵידָא דְהֶקְדֵּשׁ דְּלֹא שָׁקֵיל אֶלָּא שְׁתֵּי סְלָעִים וּשְׁנֵי פֻנְדְיוֹנִים, הָכָא וַדַּאי מְחַשְּׁבִין אוֹתָן כִּיצִיאָה גְמוּרָה וְאֵין כָּאן שְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי הַיּוֹבֵל, וְלֹא מִפַּרְקָא בְגֵרוּעַ אֶלָּא בַחֲמִשִּׁים שֶׁקֶל, דִּכְתִיב וְחִשַּׁב לוֹ הַכֹּהֵן, מִכָּל מָקוֹם:


אבל הקדש מחשב חדשים – meaning to say, if it is interval of dedication to the Temple, that we calculate it through the passing of the half year that passed, as for example, that a person sanctifies to the Temple in the half of the forty-eighth year of the Jubilee, for if you calculated for that passage, it would be less than two years before the Jubilee and we don’t redeem for a deduction from the price of the redemption according to the years of possession with due deduction other than for fifty years. But if you say that we don’t calculate the months that passed, we have here two years prior to the Jubilee, and there is loss to that which is dedicated to the Temple that he did not give other than two Sela and two Pundiyonim, here certainly we calculate them according to the complete passage of time, and there aren’t two years before the Jubilee [year], but we don’t redeem for a deduction from the price of the redemption other than for fifty shekels, as it is written (Leviticus 27:18): “the priest shall compute the price,” nonetheless.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:5)


**בִּשְׁעַת הַיּוֹבֵל.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁהַיּוֹבֵל נוֹהֵג. אֲבָל בִּזְמַן שֶׁאֵין הַיּוֹבֵל נוֹהֵג, פּוֹדֶה אוֹתָהּ בְּשָׁוְיָהּ כִּשְׁאָר הֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת:


בשעת היובל – at the time when the Jubilee is in vogue. But at the time when the Jubilee is not in vogue, he redeems it according to its value like the rest of the things dedicated to the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:6)


**זֶרַע חֹמֶר שְׂעֹרִים.** מָקוֹם שֶׁיְּכוֹלִים לִזְרֹעַ בּוֹ כּוֹר שְׂעֹרִים כְּשֶׁזּוֹרְעִים בְּמַפֹּלֶת יָד וּבִזְרִיעָה בֵּינוֹנִית, לֹא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַזֶּרַע מְקֻבָּץ בְּיוֹתֵר וְלֹא מְפֻזָּר וּמְפֹרָד הַרְבֵּה:


זרע חמר שעורים – a place where he is able to sow a KOR of barley when they sow with the quantity of seed which it takes when throwing with the hand and with an intermediate sowing, not that the seed should be pressed together greatly or spread out and dispersed a great deal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:7)


**נְקָעִים עֲמֻקִּים וְכוּ'.** וְהוּא שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מְלֵאִים מַיִם שֶׁאֵין רְאוּיִין לִזְרִיעָה. אֲבָל אִם אֵינָן מְלֵאִים מַיִם, הוֹאִיל וַחֲזוּ לְמִידִי, אַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין נִמְדָדִין עִמָּהּ מִתְחַשְּׁבִין בְּאַפֵּי נַפְשַׁיְהוּ, וְנִפְדִּין לְפִי חֶשְׁבּוֹן חֲמִשִּׁים סְלָעִים לְבֵית כּוֹר:


נקעים עמוקים וכו' (cavities that are ten handbreadths deep) – and that they would be filled with water that are not appropriate for sowing, but if they are not filled with water, for since, they are proper whatever it may be, even though they are not measured with it, we calculate them on their own, and they are redeemed according to the calculation of fifty Sela for a Bet Kor/an area requiring a Kor of seed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:8)


**פָּחוֹת מִכָּאן נִמְדָּדִין עִמָּהּ.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן מְלֵאִים מַיִם וְלֹא חֲזוּ לִזְרִיעָה, הוֹאִיל וְאֵין עֲמֻקִּים עֲשָׂרָה. וְכֵן סְלָעִים הוֹאִיל וְאֵין גְּבוֹהִים עֲשָׂרָה עַל שֵׁם הַקַּרְקַע הֵן נִקְרָאִין וְאֵינָן חֲשׁוּבִים בִּפְנֵי עַצְמָן:


פחות מכאן נמדדין עמה – even though they are filled with water and hey are not proper for sowing, since they are not ten [handbreadths] deep. And similarly rocks, since they are not ten [handbreadths] high, they are called by the name of the land [it is part of] and are not considered on their own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:1:9)


**אֶלָּא נוֹתֵן אֶת כֻּלּוֹ כְּאֶחָד.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כז) וְחִשַּׁב לוֹ הַכֹּהֵן אֶת הַכֶּסֶף, עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא כֻלּוֹ כֶּסֶף כְּאֶחָד:


אלא נותן את כולו כאחד – as it is written (Leviticus 27:18): “the priest shall compute the price” until there would be all of the money would be as one.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:2:1)


**וְאֶחָד כָּל הָאָדָם.** פּוֹדֶה בֵית כּוֹר בַּחֲמִשִּׁים שֶׁקֶל, בֵּין שֶׁהוּא שָׁוֶה אֶלֶף שְׁקָלִים בֵּין שֶׁאֵינוֹ שָׁוֶה אֶלָּא חֲמִשָּׁה שְׁקָלִים:


ואחד כל אדם – who redeems a Bet Kor/an area requiring a Kor of seed, whether it is worth one-thousand shekel or whether it is not worth anything other than fifty shekel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:2:2)


**שֶׁהַבְּעָלִים נוֹתְנִים חֹמֶשׁ.** כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) וְאִם גָּאֹל יִגְאַל אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה הַמַּקְדִּישׁ אוֹתוֹ וְגוֹ':


שהבעלים נותנים חומש – as it is written (Leviticus 27:19): “and if he who consecrated the land wishes to redeem itג, [he must add one-fifth to the sum at which it was assessed, and it will pass to him].”



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:1)


**אֵינָהּ יוֹצְאָה מִיָּדוֹ בַּיּוֹבֵל.** לִהְיוֹת מִתְחַלֶּקֶת לַכֹּהֲנִים כִּי הֵיכִי דְּאִי הֲוָה פָרִיק לַהּ אֱינַשׁ אַחֲרִינָא דַּהֲוָה נַפְקָא בַּיּוֹבֵל וּמִתְחַלֶּקֶת לַכֹּהֲנִים כְּדִכְתִיב (שם) וְהָיָה הַשָּׂדֶה בְּצֵאתוֹ בַיֹּבֵל קֹדֶשׁ לַה':


אינה יוצאה מידו ביובל – to be distributed to the Kohanim for just as that if another person had redeemed it and it go forth on the Jubilee and distributed to the Kohanim, as it is written (Leviticus 27:21): “when it is released in the jubilee, the land shall be holy to the LORD, [as land proscribed; it becomes the priest’s holding].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:2)


**גְּאָלָהּ בְּנוֹ יוֹצְאָה לְאָבִיו בַּיּוֹבֵל.** וְלֹא לַכֹּהֲנִים. דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְאִם מָכַר אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה לְאִישׁ אַחֵר לֹא יִגָאֵל עוֹד. לְאִישׁ אַחֵר, וְלֹא לַבֵּן:


גאלה בנו יוצאה לאביו ביובל – but not to the Kohanim, as it is written (Leviticus 27:20): “[But if he does not redeem the land] and the land is sold to another, it shall no longer be redeemable,” "לאיש אחר" /to another person – but not to the son.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:3)


**גְּאָלָהּ אֶחָד מִקְּרוֹבָיו.** שֶׁל מַקְדִּישׁ מִיַּד הַגִּזְבָּר, וּבָא הַמַּקְדִּישׁ וּגְאָלָהּ מִיַּד קְרוֹבוֹ, אֵינָהּ יוֹצְאָה מִיַּד הַמַּקְדִּישׁ בַּיּוֹבֵל לְהִתְחַלֵּק לַכֹּהֲנִים:


גאלה איד מקרוביו – of the person who dedicated it from the hand of the treasurer, and the person who dedicated it and redeemed it from the hand of his relative, it does not go out from the hand of the one who dedicated it in the Jubilee [year] to be distributed to the Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:4)


**גְּאָלָהּ כֹּהֵן.** מִיַּד גִּזְבָּר:


גאלה כהן – from the hand of the treasurer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:3:5)


**לֹא יֹאמַר הוֹאִיל.** וְאִם גְּאָלָהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל יוֹצְאָה לִי וְלַחֲבֵרַי בַּיּוֹבֵל, עַכְשָׁיו שֶׁהִיא תַּחַת יָדִי שֶׁגְּאַלְתִּיהָ אֵין לְךָ כֹהֵן רָאוּי לָהּ יוֹתֵר מִמֶּנִּי:


לא יאמר הגואל – but if an Israelite redeemed it, it goes out to me and to my colleagues on the Jubilee [year], now that it is under my hand that I redeemed it, there is no Kohen who is more appropriate for me than me.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:1)


**וְלֹא נִגְאֲלָה.** לֹא גְאָלוּהָ מִיַּד הַהֶקְדֵּשׁ, לֹא הַבְּעָלִים וְלֹא אַחֵר:


ולא נגאלה – it was not redeemed from the hand/domain of the Temple, neither by its owners or by anyone else.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:2)


**הַכֹּהֲנִים נִכְנָסִים לָהּ.** כֹּהֲנִים שֶׁל מִשְׁמָר שֶׁהַיּוֹבֵל פּוֹגֵעַ בּוֹ הֵן נִכְנָסִים לְתוֹכָהּ, וְהִיא חֲלוּטָה בְיָדָם:


הכהנים נכנסים לה – Kohanim of the priestly watch (one of the twenty-four such groups – each for a week at a time) when the Jubilee comes in contact with it, they enter into it, and it is irredeemable in their hands.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:3)


**וְנוֹתְנִים אֶת דָּמֶיהָ.** לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ. דְּגָמַר קֹדֶשׁ קֹדֶשׁ מִמַּקְדִּישׁ בַּיִת, כְּתִיב הָכָא בִּשְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה בְּצֵאתוֹ בַּיּוֹבֵל קֹדֶשׁ, וּכְתִיב בְּמַקְדִּישׁ בַּיִת (שם) וְאִישׁ כִּי יַקְדִּישׁ אֶת בֵּיתוֹ קֹדֶשׁ, מַה בַּיִת לֹא תֵצֵא מִיַּד הֶקְדֵּשׁ בְּלֹא דָמִים כְּדִכְתִיב הָתָם וְהֶעֱרִיכוֹ הַכֹּהֵן, דְּבַיִת לֹא נַפְקָא לַכֹּהֲנִים אֶלָּא נִפְדֶּה בְדָמִים, אַף שְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה לֹא תֵצֵא מִיַּד הֶקְדֵּשׁ בְּלֹא דָמִים. הִלְכָּךְ כִּי פָרִיק לָהּ אַחֵר, הָווּ דָמִים לְבֶדֶק הַבַּיִת הֶקְדֵּשׁ, וְכִי מָטֵי יוֹבֵל נַפְקָא בְּחִנָּם לַכֹּהֲנִים, וְאִם לֹא נִגְאֲלָה יָהֲבֵי כֹהֲנִים דְּמֵי חֲמִשִּׁים שְׁקָלִים וְשָׁקְלֵי לָהּ:


ונותנים את דמיה למקדש – for they derive/learned "קודש" (i.e., Leviticus 27:14: “If anyone consecrates his house to the LORD”) "קודש" (i.e., Leviticus 27:21: “when it is released in the jubilee it shall be holy to the LORD”) from one who dedicates/sanctifies his house. It is written here regarding an ancestral land (Leviticus 27:21): “when it is released in the jubilee, the land shall be holy to the LORD,” and it is written regarding one who consecrates his house (Leviticus 27:14): “If anyone consecrates his house to the LORD,” just as the house does not leave from the possession of the Temple without payment of money, as it is written there (Leviticus 27:14): “the priest shall assess it”, for a house does not go out to the Kohanim but rather is redeemed with money, so also the ancestral field does not go out from being devoted to the Temple without money. Therefore, when another person redeems it, the monies go to the Sanctuary for the repair of the Temple, and when the Jubilee [year] arrives, it goes out without payment to the Kohanim, and if it was not redeemed, the Kohanim give the sum of fifty Shekels and take it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר נִכְנָסִין אֲבָל לֹא נוֹתְנִין.** דְּגָמַר קֹדֶשׁ קֹדֶשׁ מִכִּבְשֵׂי עֲצֶרֶת, דִּכְתִיב בְּהוּ (ויקרא כג) קֹדֶשׁ יִהְיוּ לַה'‏ לַכֹּהֵן, מַה הָתָם בְּחִנָּם אַף כָּאן בְּחִנָּם. וְנִיחָא לֵיהּ דְּנֵילַף שְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה מִכִּבְשֵׂי עֲצֶרֶת, דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ מֵעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע מַתְּנוֹת כְּהֻנָּה, וְלֹא נֵילַף מִמַּקְדִּישׁ בֵּיתוֹ דְּאֵינוֹ נִתָּן לַכֹּהֲנִים לְעוֹלָם. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה נִיחָא לֵיהּ דְּנֵילַף שְׂדֵה אֲחֻזָּה מִמַּקְדִּישׁ בֵּיתוֹ, דְּתַרְוַיְהוּ קָדְשֵׁי בֶּדֶק הַבַּיִת, וְלֹא נֵילַף מִכִּבְשֵׂי עֲצֶרֶת שֶׁהֵם קָדְשֵׁי מִזְבֵּחַ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ר' שמעון נכנסין אבל לא נותנין – that he derived/learned "קודש" (i.e., Leviticus 23:20): “[The priest shall elevate these] – the two lambs [together with the bread of first fruits as an elevation offering before the LORD;] they shall be holy to the LORD/קדש יהיו לה', for the priest.”) "קודש" (i.e., Leviticus 27:14) from the lambs of Atzeret/Shavuot, as it is written with them (Leviticus 23:20): “they shall be holy to the LORD for the priest.” Just as there they are done gratuitously, even here, it is done gratuitously. And it is satisfactory for him that the ancestral field is derived from the lambs for Atzeret/Shavuot, for both of them are from the twenty-four gifts to the priesthood, and he doesn’t derive it from one who dedicates his home [to the Temple] and it is not given ever to the Kohanim. But for Rabbi Yehuda, it is satisfactory that the ancestral field is derived from one who dedicates/sanctifies his home [to the Temple] for both of them are the holy things devoted to the repair of the Temple. And it is not derived from the lambs for Atzeret which are the holy things of the Altar. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:5)


**שְׂדֵה רְטוּשִׁין.** שָׂדֶה עֲזוּבָה. כְּמוֹ [הוֹשֵׁעַ י] אֵם עַל בָּנִים רֻטָּשָׁה:


שדה רטושין – an abandoned/left-behind field. Like (Hosea 10:14): “When mothers and babes were dashed to death together.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Arakhin 7:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Arakhin/r/7:4:6)


**עַד שֶׁיִּגְאָלֶנָּה אַחֵר.** וּכְשֶׁתֵּצֵא מִיָּדוֹ בַּיּוֹבֵל יִכָּנְסוּ בָּהּ כֹּהֲנִים. וְטַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר, דִּכְתִיב וְהָיָה הַשָּׂדֶה בְּצֵאתוֹ בַיֹּבֵל קֹדֶשׁ, מַשְׁמַע כְּשֶׁתֵּצֵא מִיַּד הַגּוֹאֵל כְּדִין שְׁאָר אָדָם הַקּוֹנֶה קַרְקַע שֶׁיּוֹצְאָה בַּיּוֹבֵל, בְּהַהוּא קָאָמַר קְרָא דְּתֶהֱוֵי לַכֹּהֲנִים, אֲבָל בְּצֵאתוֹ מִיַּד הֶקְדֵּשׁ לֹא מַשְׁמַע, דְּאַכַּתִּי לֹא אַשְׁמוֹעִינַן קְרָא דְּהֶקְדֵּשׁ פָּקַע מִכֹּחוֹ בַּיּוֹבֵל:


עד שיגאלנה אחר – when it leaves from hand on the Jubilee, Kohanim can enter it.
